00160: def finish(name, color, scale, stretch, roughness, variation, bump_amount):
00161:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00162:     mat.use_nodes = True
00163:     nodes, links = mat.node_tree.nodes, mat.node_tree.links
00164:     bs = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00165:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00166:     tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00167:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = scale
00168:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 3
00169:     coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00170:     mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00171:     mapping.operation = "MULTIPLY"
00172:     mapping.inputs[1].default_value = stretch
00173:     links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"], mapping.inputs[0])
00174:     links.new(mapping.outputs[0], tex.inputs["Vector"])
00175:     ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00176:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (
00177:         *(c*(1-variation) for c in color), 1)
00178:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (
00179:         *(min(1, c*(1+variation)) for c in color), 1)
00180:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], ramp.inputs[0])
00181:     links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00182:     bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00183:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = bump_amount
00184:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .008
00185:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00186:     links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00187:     return mat
00188: 
00189: wood = finish("Oak vertical grain", (.43, .29, .17),
00190:               5, (9, 9, .12), .62, .43, .22)
00191: plaster = finish("Cream mineral plaster", (.79, .79, .735),
00192:                  95, (1, 1, 1), .85, .065, .10)
00193: 
00194: for ob in bpy.context.scene.objects:
00195:     if ob.type != "MESH":
00196:         continue
00197:     points = [ob.matrix_world @ Vector(p) for p in ob.bound_box]
00198:     low = Vector(tuple(min(p[k] for p in points) for k in range(3)))
00199:     high = Vector(tuple(max(p[k] for p in points) for k in range(3)))
00200:     center = (low+high)/2
00201:     dims = high-low
00202:     mat = None
00203:     if dims.z > 3.5 and dims.x < .4 and dims.y < .4:
00204:         mat = wood

00206:         mat = plaster

00128: # Fine procedural finishes; no image textures are used.
00129: def textured_material(name, color, roughness, scale, bump_strength, stretch=None):
00130:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00131:     mat.use_nodes = True
00132:     nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00133:     links = mat.node_tree.links
00134:     bs = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00135:     bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color, 1)
00136:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness

00160: def finish(name, color, scale, stretch, roughness, variation, bump_amount):
00161:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00162:     mat.use_nodes = True
00163:     nodes, links = mat.node_tree.nodes, mat.node_tree.links
00164:     bs = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00165:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00166:     tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00167:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = scale
00168:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 3
00169:     coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00170:     mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00171:     mapping.operation = "MULTIPLY"
00172:     mapping.inputs[1].default_value = stretch
00173:     links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"], mapping.inputs[0])
00174:     links.new(mapping.outputs[0], tex.inputs["Vector"])
00175:     ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00176:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (
00177:         *(c*(1-variation) for c in color), 1)
00178:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (
00179:         *(min(1, c*(1+variation)) for c in color), 1)
00180:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], ramp.inputs[0])
00181:     links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00182:     bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00183:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = bump_amount
00184:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .008
00185:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00186:     links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00187:     return mat
00188: 
00189: wood = finish("Oak vertical grain", (.43, .29, .17),
00190:               5, (9, 9, .12), .62, .43, .22)
00191: plaster = finish("Cream mineral plaster", (.79, .79, .735),
00192:                  95, (1, 1, 1), .85, .065, .10)
00193: 
00194: for ob in bpy.context.scene.objects:
00195:     if ob.type != "MESH":
00196:         continue
00197:     points = [ob.matrix_world @ Vector(p) for p in ob.bound_box]
00198:     low = Vector(tuple(min(p[k] for p in points) for k in range(3)))
00199:     high = Vector(tuple(max(p[k] for p in points) for k in range(3)))
00200:     center = (low+high)/2
00201:     dims = high-low
00202:     mat = None
00203:     if dims.z > 3.5 and dims.x < .4 and dims.y < .4:
00204:         mat = wood
00205:     elif (center-Vector(hood_center)).length < .05 and dims.z > 1.8:
00206:         mat = plaster
00207:     if mat:
00208:         ob.data.materials.clear()
00209:         ob.data.materials.append(mat)
